### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 澳洲央行決議如預期升息一碼至 4.60%為今年第四度升息，基準利率創 2011 年
以來新高，澳洲央行在政策聲明中表示通膨壓力尚未緩解，不排除在 11、12 月
政策會議進一步升息；另一方面，澳洲 8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%略低
於預期，但年增 4.0%仍高於澳洲央行 2~3%的目標區間。
⚫ 美國第二季美 GDP 終值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%，其中實質個人消費支出創下 2025
年 3 月以來最大單月增幅，顯示即使高利率持續，美國家庭消費依然具韌性；
另一方面，美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增月增 0.2%、3.0%，雙雙低於市場預
期的 0.3%、3.3%，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人，失
業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，8 月非農就業人數亦同步下修至 13.3 萬人，通膨下滑
和勞動力市場略有降溫，降低市場對聯準會 10 月升息預期，Fedwatch 顯示 10

月升息機率下降至 20%左右。
⚫ 受美伊衝突引發的能源價格衝擊影響，9 月歐元地區消費者物價調和指數(HICP)
整體上升速度遠超預期，其中，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%，歐元區年增
3.8%，均高於歐洲央行的 2%目標，反映能源價格的衝擊持續影響物價，加深歐
洲央行再次升息的壓力。
⚫ 日本上週公佈貨幣政策會議紀要及央行意見摘要顯示多名委員認為當前政策利
率依然處於中性區間之下，需警惕通膨上行風險，且未來應該以更快速度升息抑
制通膨，然而意見摘要提及日本經濟財政大臣城內實表示希望央行充分審視過
去升息對景氣之影響，市場解讀為政府牽制日本央行未來升息步伐，而財務大臣
片山皋月則重申尊重日本央行的自主性。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.760，低點在 31.946，以 31.830 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方，市場預期聯準
會後續仍有升息的空間，提振美元指數表現，加以外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣走貶
至 31.900 上方；不過適逢月底也是季底，匯價逢高吸引出口商進場賣美金，緩解台
幣貶值的速度；臨近週末，美國 8 月核心 PCE 數據 3%低於預期 3.3%，但市場等待
9 月非農就業數據，交投偏向謹慎觀望，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.830。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略有
減弱，市場對於 10 月升息的預期下降，但仍預期 12 月會再度升息。美元指數雖然
脫離高點，但仍在 102 附近高位震盪，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台股再創收盤新高且外資轉為買超台股，且隨著 AI 需求持續帶動台灣經濟出口表
現強勁，預計出口商後續拋匯需求將持續提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，預估本週台幣
區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪偏升。隨著美中兩國完成新一輪談判，雙方同意延長休兵，從
原定的 11 月延長到明年 1 月，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，支撐人民幣的走勢由
6.7170 走升至 6.7030；不過由於美伊戰事持續，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方，
加以美元指數及美債收益率雙雙走高，令人民幣由升轉貶至 6.7200 上方；臨近週末，
美國公布 9 月非農數據降溫，聯準會 10 月升息預期下滑，美元指數回落，境外人民
幣走升至 6.7000 週內高點，終場境外人民幣收在 6.7061，週線升值 166 點，境內人
民幣於十一長假前夕收盤於在 6.7053，週線升值 72 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間震盪。就國際市場來看，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，遠低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略
為減弱。數據公布後，市場對於聯準會 10 月升息的預期下降，美元指數小幅回落，
有助於人民幣的表現；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價格再度升破 6.7400，有助
於人民幣的走勢，不過 10/1~10/7 是中國十一長假，預計人民幣走勢主要將受國際美
元影響；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線仍向下發散，空頭格
局不變，不過匯價反彈至 20 日均線附近，顯示下行動能暫緩。綜合以上，預估本週
人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7300

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪上行。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，USD/JPY 大致
在 156.50 至 157.85 間波動；週間，適逢日本上半年會計年度結算，出口商拋匯使資
金匯回力道強勁，帶動 USD/JPY 下行最低來到 156.35，但逢低有買盤進場，USD/JPY
回到 157 整數關卡之上，後隨美債殖利率創高、美元指數突破 102 關卡，USD/JPY
上行最高來至 158.45；臨近週末，美國公布 9 月非農就業人數低於預期，USD/JPY
一度回落至 157 下方，終場收在 157.83，週線上漲 57 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本 9 月製造業 PMI 由 54.9
回落至 54.1，增長速度放慢至六個月以來的低位，主因為產出和新訂單增長放緩，
經濟復甦力道可能減緩，不利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%，
美國 9 月非農數據 2.9 萬人均低於預期，通膨和勞動力市場略有降溫使市場對聯準
會升息預期下滑，加之部分聯準會官員表示無需急於進一步升息，Fedwatch 顯示 10
月升息機率降至兩成，然而美國第二季 GDP 上修至 2.2%經濟基本面穩健仍將支撐
美元。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價處於區間波動，且各天期均線
趨平，上方 158.50 有年線、季線壓力，下方 156.25 則有月線支撐，預期短線以區間
震盪可能性較高。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.50-159.00 
前週交易區間 
156.35 158.45

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，EUR/USD 震盪下
行至 1.1310，而在紐約聯儲主席威廉姆斯發表不急於再次升息的偏鴿言論後，
EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1340；週間，美國 8 月 PCE 數據低於預期，EUR/USD 一度反彈
至 1.1380，然而在全球債券拋售潮下，資金回流美元帶動美元指數突破 102 關卡，
EUR/USD 跌破 1.1300 關卡最低來至 1.1214；臨近週末，美國 9 月非農就業人數低
於預期，EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1285 後回落，終場收在 1.1253，週線下跌 138 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元地區消費者
物價調和指數(HICP)，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%、歐元區年增 3.8%，顯示能
源價格衝擊依然使通膨壓力居高不下，市場預期歐洲央行可能在 12 月再度升息，將
帶給歐元部分支撐。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%、月增 0.2%低於預
期，而 9 月非農就業人數 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人，失業率則由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，
就業降溫及通膨回落降低市場對聯準會十月升息的預期，然而第二季美國 GDP 終
值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%，經濟基本面持穩仍有利美元維持強勢。三、技術面而言，在
日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價在各天期均線下方並跌破 1.1300 整數關卡，月線向下穿
過季線形成死亡交叉，顯示短線空方動能較強。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈
現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1150-1.1400 
前週交易區間 
1.1214 1.1391

### Page 7

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.29-115.10.02 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.82%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本週為提存期底，繼央行上週大砍存單效應持續發酵，市
場資金供給仍充裕，惟需求疲弱，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率高位較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投仍冷清，5 年期指標券 A15108 週三於處所成交在 1.82%，10 年
期指標券則未成交，週五等殖系統參考報價中價為 1.95%。 
第四季台債發行量雖偏少，惟美伊和談依舊陷入僵局，國際油價續處高位，通
膨疑慮尚存，國際債市走勢仍偏空，預期台債殖利率短線將維持高檔盤整格
局，預估 5 年券區間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.95%~2%。

## Document 113: 261108_ms_wiwynn

Source PDF: all report pdf/261108_ms_wiwynn.pdf

### Page 1

M
Idea
Wiwynn Corp  | Asia Pacific
3Q beat on higher GM/OpM
Recent concerns revolve around potential data center delays,
though the supply chain has not seen any changes. However, we
lower our mid-term growth  rate to bake in some conservatism.
Our PT now implies a lower CY27 P/E at  12x. RR looks attractive
at 7.7x CY27 P/E vs. the 3Y  avg. of ~10x.
4Q revenue should continue to grow double-digits q/q: Wiwynn announced
October revenue at ~NT$165B (-4% m/m, +81% y/y). If we assume the October

monthly run-rate can be sustained, 4Q should grow at least 13-14% q/q. However,
we think ASIC server racks will continue to ramp-up through-out year-end, so we
forecast 4Q revenue to ~20% q/q to reach ~NT$520B (+78% y/y). Higher cost
structure and higher mix of ASIC server racks should drive margins to decline q/q to
~8.5% (-40bps q/q), resulting in net profit of ~NT$27.7B or EPS of NT$46.70.
ASIC/GPU projects remain on track: Our checks indicate that  Trainium3 server rack

production is ramping up well at Wiwynn, and with T3, Wiwynn is a more
comprehensive partner, also engaging in UBB and switch trays. It will also engage in
AMD's (covered by Joe Moore) MI455 Helios rack assembly for customers such as
Meta (covered by Brian Nowak) and Oracle (covered by Sanjit Singh). We forecast
Wiwynn to build ~8K Helios racks for CY27.
3Q results beat on better margins: Revenue was NT$434.6B (+63% y/y) with GM
of 8.9% (-40bps q/q), which is 90bps/30bps above MSe/Street. OP reached ~NT

$32.5B (+66% y/y) for OpM of 7.5% (+20bps q/q), which is 100bps/50bps above
MSe/Street, driven by operating leverage. Non-op losses were slightly higher than
MSe/Street mainly due to higher interest expense, resulting in net profit of ~NT
$23.4B (+52% y/y) for EPS of NT$42.09, 44%/10% above MSe/Street.
Reiterate OW with price target unchanged at NT$2,515 (12x/11x CY27/28e P/E) –
our higher EPS estimates are offset by a lower mid-term growth rate. Risk-reward is

highly attractive at 7.7x CY27e or 6.7x CY28e P/E vs. the three-year range of 6-16x,
with a strong pipeline of AI and non-AI server projects.

Key Takeaways
3Q EPS was NT$42.09, 44% above our estimate/10% above the Street, driven by
GM/OpM beat despite higher non-op losses.

Oct rev at ~NT$165M (+81% y/y), implies 4Q rev should grow double-digits q/q,
driven by continued strength from general server and rising ASIC server racks.
November 8, 2026 08:46 PM GMT

Key changes in our estimates 
Please see below where we highlight our changes to revenue estimates to Wiwynn from 
key hyperscaler customers. These are a combination of ASP and volume changes, based 
on our latest Asia supply chain checks. We also include assumptions from AMD Helios 
racks assembly into our CY27 estimates, at around ~8K racks.

4
Our overall earnings estimate changes
We raise our EPS estimates 13% for CY26, 29% for CY27, and 29% for CY28: This
mainly reflects changes to our assumptions, which we laid out in the section above. The
difference between net profit and EPS change is due to changes in share count.

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
Price target discussion and valuation methodology
We keep our price target unchanged at NT$2,515: This implies 12x CY27e P/E or 11x CY28
P/E, vs. its thee-year average forward P/E of 10.3x, which we think is fair, in view of
Wiwynn's AI server project wins and share gains for general-purpose servers in the next 1-
2 years.
We continue to use a residual income model to derive our price target. Key assumptions
include a cost of equity of 7.0% (beta of 1.0, equity premium of 6% and risk-free rate of

1%), a medium-term growth rate of 4% (lowered from 6%), and a terminal growth rate of
3%.
Our bull and bear case scenario values are also unchanged at NT$3,210 and NT$1,470,
respectively.

MS Rating
▪ Beneﬁciary of enlarged CPU TAM driven

by rising demand for agentic AI.
▪ Share gainer within ASIC programs, with
Wiwynn taking share on UBB and switch
tray assembly, along with ramp up of
Trainium 3 from 2H26.
▪ Wiwynn will also be the main ODM for
Meta and Oracle's AMD MI4505 Helios
server racks, which will mainly contribute in
CY27.
▪ It will also be a supplier of Nvidia VR200
racks for Oracle, which will mainly
contribute in CY27.
▪ Our price target implies 12x CY27e P/E,
not stretched vs. the three-year average of

~10x.

16x 2027e P/E
Data center builds accelerate: Assumes
geopolitical tensions diminish, with macro
data picking up, and accelerating data center

further demand upside for general
computing servers and AI servers alike.
NT$2,515.00
12x 2027e P/E
Based on our analysis, we now expect AI
servers to account for ~50% of revenue in
2027, from 20-30% in 2024, as Wiwynn
ramps up its AI server shipments and
general server demand also grows strongly.
NT$1,470.00
7.3x 2027e P/E
Data center builds get pushed out: Assumes
geopolitical tensions escalate, with macro
data deteriorating, driving hyperscale

vendors such as Meta and Microsoft to push
out their data center builds, which leads to
further demand downside for both general
computing servers and AI servers.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Simplo Technology (6121.TWO)
An Established Battery Franchise with Room for a Better Mix
CITI'S TAKE 
3Q26 EPS missed our/BBG estimates by 35%/30% each as notebook pull-
ins faded and low yields on a major US customer’s new smartphone held 
back shipments. We expect higher PC component costs to keep IT demand 
subdued in 1Q27E, while rising contribution from AES could still help margin 
expansion in 2027E. We lower 2026-2028E EPS by 17-20% and revise our 
TP to NT$400 based on 12x 2027E EPS, lowered from 13x reflecting weaker 
visibility on IT recovery. We continue to favor Simplo’s leading market share 
in notebook/smartphone battery franchises and its exposure to AIDC BBU 
growth through AES. 
3Q earnings -17% y/y missed our/BBG estimates by 35%/30% on weaker IT 
shipments — 3Q26 sales of NT$19.9bn fell 2% y/y and 5% q/q, 13%/10% below 
our/BBG forecasts. Notebook customers pulled orders into 1H26 amid rising 
memory prices, while lower yields on a major US customer’s new smartphone 
constrained 3Q shipment. Group GPM rose 0.7ppt y/y to 16.3% on a larger AES 
contribution. However, standalone GPM dropped 1.6ppt y/y 50 9.2% on higher input 
costs and lower volumes. Group OPM rose 0.4ppt y/y to 9.7%, lower than our (12.2%) 
and BBG (11.9%) estimates. Higher taxes also weighed on EPS. 
2027E outlook stays muted for IT — The mgmt. expects IT demand to remain 
lukewarm in 1Q27E as higher component costs put upward pressure on PC prices 
likely weighing on demand. Standalone sales are expected to be flat to slightly down 
y/y in 2027E, with the group sales flat to slightly up on AES’s BBU growth. Mgmt. 
notes the battery-pack inventories at notebook brands are lean at just a few weeks 
and easing cobalt prices could bring down ASP in 2027E. A new non-IT product line 
is growing severalfold from a small base, and the mgmt. expects it to make up >5% 
in 2028E with a GPM above group’s average. 
Implications — We cut 2026-2028E earnings estimates 17-20% for 2026-2028E 
reflecting 3Q miss and a slower IT recovery, resulting in a 10-18% cut on our sales 
forecasts. We lower our TP to NT$400 based on a target P/E of 12x from 13x on 2027E 
EPS, reflecting the component inflation likely delaying the timing of IT recovery. We 
stay Buy on Simplo’s established notebook and smartphone battery franchises and 
AES’s exposure to growing datacenter BBU demand. AI PC adoption could lift 
battery-pack ASPs over time, and an estimated 6% dividend yield should help 
cushion downside.

Simplo Technology (6121.TWO)
09 November 2026 Citi Research

Simplo Technology
Company description
Established in 1992, Simplo specializes in designing and producing battery
packs for Notebooks, tablet PC, and electronic vehicle products. With strong
execution and cost advantages, Simplo continues to broaden its client base.
Its major customers include PC OEMs (such as HPQ, Dell, Asus, Acer, and
Apple) and NB ODMs (such as Quanta, Compal, Wistron, and Inventec).

Investment strategy
We rate Simplo shares as Buy. Simplo’s heating fundamentals are driven by
the following reasons. 1) Japan supplier's strategy change on focusing EV
battery pack and de-prioritizing NB which provides Simplo more orders wins;
and 2)  Non-IT: according to our industry survey, Simplo has been qualified by
global ISPs for the battery back units (BBU) for their data centers.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on a target P/E of 12x 2027E EPS, on par

with its historical average since 2010.
Risks
Downside risks that may cause the shares to continue to trade below our
target price include: 1) weaker than expected end product sales; 2) intensified
competition; and 3) worse-than-expected overall NB/tablet/PC growth.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 116: 261109_daiwa_ Giant Manufacturing

Source PDF: all report pdf/261109_daiwa_ Giant Manufacturing.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: Giant announced its 3Q26 results after market hours on 9 
November 2026. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating on its improving 
fundamentals and the recovery of the global bike market. We believe 
inventory destocking is coming to an end.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results: missed on operating margin. Giant’s 
3Q26 revenue of TWD16.311bn (-2.1% QoQ, +6.2% YoY) was roughly in 
line with our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus. Its gross margin of 
23.9% beat our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus by 2.9pp and 
1.1pp, respectively, driven by product mix optimisation and new product 
launches in its own brand. However, its operating margin of 3.1% missed 
our estimate and the consensus by 1.4pp and 2.1pp, respectively, due to 
marketing activities for MY27 launches and legal fees related to the WRO. 
Thus, its 3Q26 operating profit of TWD510m (-49.0% QoQ, +28.9% YoY) 
missed our estimate and the consensus by 31.3% and 42.2%, respectively. 
Its 3Q26 net profit of TWD330m (-48.0% QoQ, -5.7% YoY) also missed our 
estimate and the consensus by 39.6% and 47.8%, respectively. Its 3Q26 
EPS finished at TWD0.84.

Demand recovery in Europe and China; a bright 2027. Giant sees 
gradual market recovery in Europe on new model launches and demand 
recovery in China. In the US, Giant continues to work towards having the 
WRO lifted, but will adjust production allocation to maximise the supply to 
meet demand in the US market. Its E-bike revenue accounted for 21% of 
9M26 revenue, which we believe reflects the declining OEM segment. 
Giant aims for its e-bike shipments to decline by 1% YoY, from 516,000 
units in 2025 to 510,000 in 2026, on weaker OE customer demand. For 
2026, it guides for sales to decline by low-single-digit % YoY, with the gross 
margin improving to 21-23% (from 20-21%) and an operating margin of 1-
3%. We lower our 2026-28E EPS by 5-30% after factoring in the 3Q26 
results with more conservative operating-margin estimates on marketing 
expenses.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call with an unchanged 
12M TP of TWD121, still based on our 1-year forward EPS forecast with an 
unchanged target PER of 22x to reflect its improved earnings trajectory, 
and we look for a better year in 2027. Key downside risks: slower-than-
expected e-bike penetration in the EU and macro/tariff uncertainties.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 8-26% below the consensus, likely 
as our revenue and operating margin forecasts are more conservative.

9 November 2026

3Q26 results review: missed on operating margin

➢ 3Q26 net profit missed our estimate and the consensus by 39-48% 
➢ Better-than-expected recovery in Europe and China; a brighter 2027 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD121

Company profile 
Founded in 1972 and listed in 1994 on Taiwan’s main board, Giant is the largest bicycle maker in 
Taiwan, with an annual capacity of c.5m bicycles. In 2025, Asia accounted for 57% of its revenue, 
followed by Europe (33%) and America (7%). Around 74% of its 2025 revenue came from OBM (Giant, 
Liv, momentum) brands and 26% from OEM/ODM (Trek, Scott and Colnago) brands.

### Page 5

5

Giant Manufacturing (9921 TT): 9 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 Aug 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and Chairperson are not the same individual. Led by the Liu family, there are 11
members on the board. The founder and former Chairman is now a director, with his niece, Mrs.
Tu, leading Giant. We view Mrs. Tu's leadership to be strong, as she is well-recognised by the
former Chairman and the board for her capabilities, and she is one of the few female
chairpersons to lead a Taiwanese company.
Capital management 2 Giant has maintained its dividend payout ratio between 50% and 100% over the past decade , which we believe to be reasonable considering its global leadership status and expansion needs.
Related party &
transaction 2
Revenue from related-party transactions accounted for 58-62% of total revenue over 2019-25,
with sales mostly from its OBM (ASP varies with products/markets). We observed no suspicious
activity.
S Product quality & safety 1
According to Giant's 2023 ESG report, Giant as the global OEM/OBM leader in the bicycle
industry, product quality and safety are the top priority. We see ongoing R&D expenses and
relevant investment in new models and note that Giant has introduced its "Right Ride" system to
assist customers seeking suitable bike options.
S Supply chain management 1
Giant has developed its Giant production management system that diversifies its capacity
globally and utilises production resources fully to lower political or regional risk. As Giant has
control over its material sourcing, design process and manufacturing/OEM, we believe it has
solid supply chain management capabilities that allow for better cost control than peers.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

Gold Circuit

Reiterate Buy: 3Q26 margin beat; ongoing 
sales/margin growth; trim PO to NT$1,250

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 1,250 TWD | Price: 983.00 TWD

3Q26 beat: solid margins despite lighter sales 
Gold Circuit (GCE)’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 38.7%/30.6% exceeded BofAe of 35.9%/27.8% 
and consensus of 36.9%/28.9%, despite lighter sales (+22% QoQ, +67% YoY; 5%/3% 
below BofAe/consensus). OP (+48% QoQ, +95% YoY) was 4%/3% above 
BofAe/consensus. We attribute the strong core results to a still favorable mix and lower 
material cost burden. In 3Q26, AI made up 50%+ of sales, vs. 30-40%/40-50% in 
1Q/2Q26. Server/networking comprised 77%/17% of 3Q26 sales, vs. 72-78%/15-20% in 
1Q-2Q26. With a non-operating loss (details to be disclosed; likely FX loss in our view) 
amid a lighter tax rate, NP was 9%/7% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3).  
Sales momentum intact; margin expansion continues 
We now expect 4Q26/1Q27 sales to rise 2%/3% QoQ & 2027/28 sales to rise 41%/31% 
YoY, driven by ASP growth (for spec upgrade and higher material cost) and ongoing 
capacity expansion in Taiwan/Thailand/China. GCE’s Oct sales (+6% MoM, +76% YoY) 
reinforce our confidence in its order and project pipeline. We also note that the impacts 
from AWS’ project pipeline change and shipping disruptions due to the flood in Thailand 
are fading. We now assume GM to expand from 36.8% in 2026E to 38.0%/38.3% in 
2027/28E, after considering higher material cost pass-through, higher depreciation costs 
from new facilities, and higher upfront costs for making next-generation HDI products.  
Cut PO to NT$1,250 with a lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We trim 2026-27E EPS by 1-4% but lift 2028E EPS by 4%, mainly to reflect the 3Q26 
results beat, stronger margin profile, but higher share count. We lower our PO to 
NT$1,250 (21x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,500 (25x 2027E EPS), with the valuation base 
unchanged and a lower P/E multiple. We apply 21x (c.1.5SD above P/E average since 
2020), vs. prior 25x (2.5SD above) on valuation de-rating across AI supply chain amid 
macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on GCE as a top ASIC server PCB 
maker, with all-time focus on high-end AI server/networking and expanding margins.

Company Sector 
Electronics

Company Description 
Gold Circuit (GCE) is a PCB company in Taiwan with high 
exposure in server (74% of 2025 sales) and networking 
(15%). Key end-clients are global CSPs and US top 
networking/telecom names. Besides its headquarters, the 
company has production sites in Taoyuan (Taiwan), Suzhou 
(China), Jiangsu (China), and Thailand, with ongoing capacity 
expansion. GCE was established in 1981 and listed on the 
Taiwan stock market in March 1998.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Gold Circuit, given (1) its high 
exposure and focus on better-margin server/networking 
business amid rising AI applications globally to keep high 
ASP/margin, (2) strong competitive advantages and high 
entry barrier as focusing on high-end products, (3) higher 
production flexibility after adding capacity in Thailand, (4) 
positive FCF/OCF since 2007/2019, respectively, with an 
improving balance sheet (from net debt to net cash) and 
expanding ROE.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Gold Circuit (GOCCF) 
Our PO of NT$1,250 is based on 21x 2027E EPS. The 21x valuation multiple is around 
1.5SD above the average level since 2020 when better-margin server business started to 
account for 50%+ of total sales, leading to an improved 20%+ GM profile. We believe 
the P/E multiple target can be justified by (1) strong AI PCB market demand, (2) GCE's 
high AI server exposure, (3) solid sales mix from better-margin server/networking 
business, and (4) ongoing margin expansion to historical high level.

Upside risks: (1) stronger server/networking market demand to drive ASP/margins, (2) 
better utilization rate, (3) faster capacity expansion in Thailand, (4) lower raw 
material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) favorable FX movement.

Downside risks: (1) ASIC platform delay, (2) weaker market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) softer AI server demand due to AI chip short supply, (4) GM pressure 
from lower utilization rate, higher raw material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) unfavorable 
FX movement.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings. 
                        Risks to Upside                    
n Stronger-than-expected AI demand

n Stronger-than-expected CPO development

Risks to Downside                    
n Slower-than-expected AI demand
n Slower-than-expected market share gain from peers

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 189: 4连涨！短期美债收益率创2022年以来最快涨幅，市场押注美联储加息

Source PDF: all report pdf/4连涨！短期美债收益率创2022年以来最快涨幅，市场押注美联储加息.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美国短期国债收益率连续四个交易⽇⼤幅攀升， 2 年期收益率升⾄ 4.862% ，累计涨幅 38.8
个基点，创 2022 年 6 ⽉以来最⼤四⽇涨幅。 10 年期收益率同步突破 5.10% 关键阈值。实际
利率同步攀升，市场已将终端利率预期⼤幅上调⾄ 4.82% ， 12 ⽉加息基本已成定局， 1 ⽉再
度加息的概率则⾼达 75% 。
摘要：
美国短期国债收益率在四个交易⽇内完成了⾃ 2022 年加息周期以来最⼤幅度的跳升，市场正在
重新定价美联储政策预期 —— 押注利率将更⾼、更持久。
周四，对美联储政策最为敏感的 2 年期美债收益率升⾄ 4.862% ，过去四个交易⽇（不计周三退
伍军⼈节休市）累计上⾏ 38.8 个基点，创下⾃ 2022 年 6 ⽉ 15 ⽇以来最⼤四交易⽇涨幅。
与此同时， 10 年期美债收益率也突破 5.10% ，刷新⼼理关键阈值，对美股构成压⼒。
此轮抛售的导⽕索接连引爆： 10 ⽉美国 CPI 数据⾼于预期、劳动⼒市场依然⽕热，美联储理事沃
勒周⼆暗⽰ 12 ⽉或需加息 50 个基点，克利夫兰联储主席 Hammack 重申政策须转向限制性⽴场，

加之地缘政治升温推⾼油价，三重压⼒叠加，加速了国债市场的抛售。
TD Securities 美国利率策略主管 Gennadiy Goldberg 表⽰：
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

通胀黏性与鹰派官员合⼒施压
周四引发市场震荡的，是多重因素的密集共振。
其⼀， 10 ⽉ CPI 数据的强劲令市场担忧通胀依然⼴泛且具黏性，进⽽质疑美联储当前渐进式加息
节奏是否⾜以应对通胀压⼒。
其⼆，多位美联储官员持续释放鹰派信号，克利夫兰联储主席 Hammack 上⽉曾投票⽀持加息，
当天重申货币政策必须转⼊限制性区间，⽅能将通胀压回 2% ⽬标。
其三，地缘政治局势的新进展推升油价，进⼀步强化了通胀⻛险叙事，加速了国债抛售。
三重因素叠加，当⽇美债收益率单⽇上⾏ 12 ⾄ 13 个基点，其中⻓端表现相对抗跌，但 10 年期收
益率仍上⾏约 8 个基点；⼀场疲弱的 30 年期国债标售结果更是雪上加霜，令⻓端收益率进⼀步承
压， 10 年期收益率最终升破 5.10% 。
实际利率同步攀升，加息预期⼤幅上移
此轮债市重定价幅度显著。
TD Securities 美国利率策略主管 Gennadiy Goldberg 认为，交易员周四已将 " 终端利率 " 预期从数
⽇前的 4.44% ⼤幅上调⾄ 4.82% ，并开始押注美联储在明年 1 ⽉前将累计再加息两次 —— 其中 12

⽉加息基本已成定局， 1 ⽉再度加息的概率则⾼达 75% 。
此次收益率急升本质上是市场对美联储预期重新定价的结果，反映出未来数⽉更为鹰派的政策⾛
向。

### Page 3

美联储今年 9 ⽉时隔三年⾸度重启加息，将联邦基⾦利率⽬标区间上调⾄ 3.75% ⾄ 4% 。
此前⼀轮⾃ 2022 年初启动、从接近零利率出发的激进加息周期，曾将利率上限推⾄ 2023 ⾄ 2024
年间的 5.5% 峰值，彼时的加息节奏为 1987 年以来最快。
值得注意的是，此轮收益率上⾏并⾮单纯由通胀预期驱动。10 年期通胀保值债券（ TIPS ）实际
收益率周四同样⼤幅跳升，连续第五个交易⽇上⾏，年初⾄今累计涨幅达 90 个基点。
分析认为，实际收益率的同步攀升意味着，市场的抛售逻辑不仅仅是对通胀的担忧，更折射出
投资者对美联储将实施真实货币紧缩的坚定预期。这对⻛险资产的压⼒，或将较单纯的通胀预
期升温更为持久。
沃什⻛格下的⾼波动时代
此轮债市动荡，也是美联储主席沃什上任以来市场⽣态转变的缩影。
沃什于今年 5 ⽉接任美联储主席⼀职，主张联储减少前瞻性沟通、更多地跟随市场信号⾏事，其
⻛格与前任形成鲜明对⽐。他还计划明年减少⼀次联邦公开市场委员会议息会议。
这种更为低调、被动的沟通⽅式，已被市场解读为政策不确定性上升的信号，并在⼀定程度上

放⼤了债市对于数据和官员⾔论的价格敏感性。

### Page 4

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

3Q26 Review: “조삼모사” Again  
3Q26P 연결 실적은 매출액 6조 943억원(YoY -6.0%), 영업이익 4,520억원(YoY -46.2%, OPM 
7.4%)을 기록하며 컨센서스 대비 연결 매출액(7.5조원)과 영업이익(1.16조원) 모두 대폭 하회했다. 주
요 자회사가 양호한 실적을 기록했음에도 불구하고, 지상방산 실적이 매우 부진했다. 지상방산은 매출
액 1조 2,455억원(YoY -41.0%), 영업이익 217억원(YoY -96.2%, OPM 1.7%)을 기록했는데, 1)3Q 
생산 물량이 4Q 매출 인식되는 흐름 속, 고정비 부담이 컸었고, 2)낮은 수출 비중(3Q 48%, 2Q 58%)
과 국내외 사업의 부진한 믹스(국내 BEP 수준, 해외 OPM 11% 추정), 3)비우호적인 원/달러 환율, 4)
대전 사업장 사고 복구 및 기타 일회성 비용(300억원+160억원, 총 460억원) 등이 반영되었다.   
대전 사업장 정상화 시점과 사업 차질 영향은? 
대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 중 정상화될 예정이며, 보은과 여수 공장에서 생산 보완을 위한 조치가 진
행 중인 것으로 소통했다. 또한 대전 사업장은 국내 물량 위주이며, 수출 납기(천무, 천궁-II 추진체 
등)에는 지장이 없을 것이라고 언급했지만, 기타 사업장 램프업을 위한 인건비 증가 등, 수익성 악화는 
불가피할 것으로 예상된다.  
15%대의 매출 성장 가이던스는 달성이 가능할까? 
보수적으로 접근하는 것이 합리적이라는 판단이다. 당사는 ‘26E 지상방산 매출액을 일부 ‘27~28E로 
이연하면서 -6.1% 하향 조정했다. 사측은 폴란드 EC1 천무 인도 가이던스를 기존 40문에서 60문으
로 상향 조정하는 등 납품 속도가 예정대비 빨라진 것을 언급했다. 다만, 천무 EC1에서 미사일 물량 
비중이 높은 것으로 파악되기 때문에 생산 지연 가능성과 수출입 데이터 상 12월 출하 물량의 매출 인
식 이월 가능성을 반영했다. ‘27E는 폴란드 후속 사업과 루마니아 K9, 호주 레드백 등이 양산 페이즈
에 진입하며 성장세가 지속될 전망이다.    
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 1,480,000원 유지 
실적 쇼크와는 별개로, 미국 K9MH 시제, 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등 수주는 서프라이즈라고 판
단된다. 분기말 수주잔고는 35조원(수출 비중 74%, 스페인 미포함)으로 중장기 성장성에 대한 이견은 
없다. 불가피한 분기 실적 변동으로 동사의 미래 가치를 예단하는 것은 시기상조라는 판단이다.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 
2026-10-14

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 820,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 366,500 원 
상승여력: 123.7%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 및 환율 영향으로 시장 예상치 하회 예상 
Key: 4Q26 부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 계약 수주 모멘텀 기대 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 4 행정 엔진&FDC 으로 주가 상승여력 
충분. 매수

3Q26 Preview: 컨센서스 하회하는 실적 기록 예상 
3Q26 연결 매출액 6조 464억원(+36.9% YoY, -4.5% QoQ), 영업이익 9,230억
원(+65.6% YoY, -11.2% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(1조 70억원)를 하회하는 실적 기
록할 것으로 추정한다. 이전 분기 대비 중대재해 및 하계휴가, 그리고 추석 연휴에 따른 
조업일수 감소 효과와 더불어 큰 폭의 환율 하락 영향(약 500 억원) 때문이다. 홍범도
함 화재 및 임단협 관련 일회성 비용은 이번 분기가 아닌 4Q 에 반영될 것으로 예상한
다. 해양 부문에서 트리온 FPU 프로젝트 C/O 에 따른 일부 환입 발생한 것으로 추정
되며, 해양 도크에서 컨테이너선 선체 블록 일부 건조로 고정비 감소효과 일부 상쇄 중
이다. 다만 올해 연내 혹은 내년 초 추가 해양 수주 없을 시 고정비 상승 우려 확대될 
전망이다. 동사 올해 상선 수주 목표(114.7 억불)를 이미 초과하는 수주 실적 기록한 상
황이며, 고마진 선종인 LNGC 및 VLGC/VLAC 중심으로 수주했기 때문에 ‘28~’29
년 구간 경쟁사 대비 뚜렷한 선종 믹스 효과를 기대한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 82 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 97 만원에서 82
만원으로 하향한다. 중국의 공격적인 캐파 증설 지속과 더불어 중국 대형 조선사들의 
글로벌 선주가 발주한 LNGC 물량 및 원가 경쟁력 확대에 따른 상선 부문의 멀티플 
조정이 불가피하다는 판단이다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클
과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지
속되고 있는 중으로 파악된다. 올해 4 분기부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 부문의 본격적
인 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 전망이며, 해당 모멘텀에 따른 주가 상승
여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)를 유지한다.

• Average consumer spend across 
the Westpac-DataX Consumer 
Panel remained resilient, rising 
1.5% in Q3 and 4.6%yr. 
• While some of this reflected a price-
driven surge in fuel spend (+0.3%qtr)

to a reduction in fuel consumption.

Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel
The Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel is a large 
dataset that gives a timely and detailed picture 
of Australian consumer finances and behaviour. 
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics 
team, the dataset links transaction activity with 
balance sheet information to give a complete view 
of income, spending, saving and borrowing flows. 
All data is de-identified and aggregated to 
ensure privacy. The resulting sample of over 
one million customers gives an accurate 
representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer and is perfectly suited 
to quickly tracing responses to events like 
interest rate moves and energy price shocks.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Heatmap of monthly spending categories
The Westpac DataX Consumer Panel shows average
consumer spending remained resilient in Q3, rising 1.5%
despite a further lift in fuel costs. Fuel contributed 0.3ppts
to quarterly growth, but discretionary spending did more

of the heavy lifting, contributing 0.9ppts as recreation
and travel-related spending recovered. Households also
continued to add to their financial buffers: savings up 1.3%
in the quarter and average buffers across households with
a mortgage increasing to 22.2 months of repayments.
Beneath the aggregate picture, lower-buffer households
remain more exposed to higher fuel costs and mortgage
repayments, but their discretionary spending share is
looking increasingly similar to other households.

Spending holds up despite higher fuel costs
Average household spending increased 1.5% in Q3, with much
of the strength again driven by transport-related spending.
Fuel contributed 0.3ppts to quarterly growth, while transport
excluding fuel contributed a further 0.4ppts. Excluding the
direct effects of the price-driven lift in fuel, average spend
was up 1.2%qtr. Importantly, stronger fuel spending did not
come at the expense of discretionary purchases. Discretionary

categories contributed 0.9ppts to quarterly spending growth,
compared with 0.5ppts from non-fuel essentials.
Solid rise in non-fuel spend and financial buffers
Discretionary spending dominates
Recreation and culture contributed 0.3ppts to growth, reflecting
stronger spending on sporting services, while restaurants and
hotels added a further 0.2ppts. Transport spending was also
boosted by a rebound in airline expenditure, which more than
unwound the weakness recorded in Q2 when refunds relating

to cancellations due to conflict in the Middle East weighed on
the category. Discretionary spending rose 0.9%qtr, following a
flat result and a 0.1% rise in the previous two quarters.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Savings growth by mortgage status
Households continue to rebuild savings
Savings increased 1.3% in Q3, with the strongest gains again 
recorded among younger households. Savings balances for 
18–34 year olds increased 2.0%qtr, compared with 0.7% 
for those aged 55 and over. Mortgagors also continued to 
accumulate savings more rapidly, recording a 2.8% increase 
compared with 0.7% among non-mortgagors. Savings growth 
among mortgagors has now outpaced non-mortgagors for 
three consecutive years.
Stronger savings have continued to lift mortgage buffers. 
Average buffers (savings divided by the sum of essential 
expenses and minimum mortgage repayments), increased to 
22.2 months of repayments in September. Looking within the 
unit-record sample, median mortgage buffers jumped from 
6.3 months in August to 6.8 months, its highest level since the 
series began in 2019.

Median mortgage buffers
Fuel spending a clear drag on consumers
Fuel spending as a share of total spending
Spending outcomes continued to vary across age groups. 
The squeeze remains most apparent among younger 
households. Fuel spending rose 5.1%qtr for 18–34 year olds, 
against a 1.5%qtr increase in non-fuel spending. By contrast, 
non-fuel spending increased 2.1%qtr among households aged 
55 and over. 
The gap between mortgagors and non-mortgagors was 
more modest. Spending rose 1.6%qtr among mortgagors 
and 1.9%qtr among those without a mortgage, suggesting 
mortgage status alone masks considerable variation in 
household financial positions – a divide that becomes clearer 
when households are grouped by the size of their mortgage 
buffers (see discussion further on in this note). Note that ‘non 
mortgagors’ covers a diverse range of consumers including 
those that are renting and those that own their homes without 
a mortgage.

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Median litres of fuel consumed
More importantly, their consumption appears to be less 
responsive to price movements. During periods of cheaper 
fuel, high-buffer households show a noticeably larger lift in 
litres purchased, consistent with stockpiling when prices are 
favourable. Low-buffer households show much less of this 
response, while their fuel volumes have also adjusted less 
during the recent increase in fuel costs.
The spending data show the consequence. Fuel expenditure 
has risen sharply across all three groups recently, but remains 
highest among low-buffer households, where it also absorbs 
a larger share of total spending. Food spending has also 
declined since 2023. This may partly reflect households 
economising within the category, including switching towards 
cheaper or home-brand products, although further work is 
needed to test this.
Taken together, the households with the smallest savings 
cushions face higher mortgage repayments, spend more 
on fuel and appear to have less scope to adjust their fuel 
consumption when prices change. 
Discretionary spend as a share of total
This suggests that in aggregate, household balance sheets 
are looking healthy. The picture is a little at odds with the very 
weak reads on our Westpac-Melbourne Index Consumer 
Sentiment Index which suggests finances are coming under 
much more pressure. The fact that both average spending and 
saving both rose suggests incomes over the quarter were not 
overly stretched and that more of the weakness in sentiment 
may relate to concerns about the outlook (something that may 
also be encouraging consumers to build buffers now, while they 
still can).
Pressures intense for low-buffer households
Aggregate buffer measures mask sizeable differences across 
mortgagors. We can get a sense of these variations by 
combining mortgagors into three broad groups according to 
buffer size, i.e. low, middle and high buffer groups. This shows 
that households with the smallest buffers are also more 
exposed to both interest rates and fuel costs.

The buffer differences are relatively stark, the median savings 
of the low-buffer group just $2k compared to $15k for the 
middle-buffer group and $95k for the high buffer group. 
Low buffer households face median minimum mortgage 
repayments of around $2,600 a month, compared with $2,400 
for the middle buffer group and $2,200 for the highest-buffer 
group. 
Low buffer households also spend more on fuel, at around 
$170 a month compared with $160 for the middle buffer 
group and $150 for the high buffer group.

We can use average pump prices to estimate what this 
expenditure implies for fuel volumes. Low-buffer households 
consume around 70 litres a month, middle-buffer households 
67 litres a month and the high-buffer group around 62 litres. 
This compares to an average of 81, 80 and 70 respectively 
through 2024-25. 
Median fuel spend by customer

### Page 5

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Yet despite this greater exposure, the gap in broader spending 
behaviour has narrowed. High-buffer households continue 
to spend more on discretionary categories, averaging around 
$1,940 a month compared with $1,740 for low-buffer 
households. However, their discretionary share has been 
broadly stable since 2023, while the shares of low- and 
middle-buffer households have risen by close to a percentage 
point bringing them more in line with the high-buffer group.

Some of the convergence reflects a longer run down-trend in 
fuel spending that has been more pronounced amongst low 
and middle buffer households. This lowering in fuel spend lifts 
the share available for other categories.

Essential spending excluding fuel, meanwhile, has changed 
little across the three cohorts since 2023. The shift in 
spending patterns has largely occurred on the discretionary 
side.

The result is a somewhat mixed picture. Low-buffer 
households remain considerably more exposed to mortgage 
and fuel costs and have far smaller savings cushions. But their 
discretionary spending has nevertheless moved closer to that 
of better-buffered households, suggesting some recovery in 
spending flexibility despite these constraints. 
Conclusion
Aggregate household finances remain resilient: spending and 
savings both increased in Q3 and mortgage buffers continued 
to rebuild. Discretionary spending has also held up despite the 
sharp increase in fuel costs.

But aggregate resilience masks a much more uneven 
distribution of pressure. Low-buffer households 
simultaneously face higher mortgage repayments, greater fuel 
exposure and much smaller savings cushions. While these 
households have so far maintained discretionary spending, 
their capacity to absorb further pressure is clearly more 
limited.
About the Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics service for 
institutional and business banking customers, the dataset links 
transaction activity with balance sheet information to give 
a complete view of income, spending, saving and borrowing 
flows. 
The resulting sample of over one million customers provides 
an accurate representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer. Monthly observations are available back 
to January 2019 with timely updates provided a few weeks 
after the end of each month. As such, it is perfectly suited to 
quickly tracing the impact and responses to events like interest 
rate moves and energy price shocks.

## Document 309: Double Feature_ The Spy Who Loved Me & To China With Love

Source PDF: all report pdf/Double Feature_ The Spy Who Loved Me & To China With Love.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Double Feature: The Spy Who Loved Me & To China With Love
Date:    8 hours ago
Double Feature: The Spy Who Loved Me & To China With Love

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Double Feature: The Spy Who
Loved Me & To China With Love
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 12 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Vows on the Move / Unsplash

I. Jensen Versus The AI Trade
Charlie Munger famously warned that there are three ways smart men can
go broke: "Liquor, ladies, and leverage." NVIDIA co-founder and now
former CEO Jensen Huang has run into trouble with the second of
Munger's three L's. He won't go broke, but he is out of a good job.
On Thursday, NVIDIA announced that Huang had resigned as CEO after
33 years at the helm of the company he co-founded in 1993. His

### Page 2

departure followed revelations of extramarital affairs, including a reported
relationship with a Chinese businesswoman suspected by US intelligence
officials of spying for Beijing. Her Singapore-based company, Megaspeed,
allegedly purchased NVIDIA's high-powered GPU chips and re-exported
them to China, violating US export restrictions. The longtime CFO Colette
Kress was appointed interim CEO while the board searches for a
successor.
Huang's departure rattled the AI trade, with NVIDIA shares plunging 4.3%,

sending its forward P/E to 11.7 (charts). We promised you buying
opportunities after the midterm elections. There were a bunch last
Thursday and a few more today.

### Page 3

Competitors AMD and Broadcom advanced 5.9% and 5.5%, respectively,
on expectations of market-share gains (chart)
As a result, the Nasdaq Composite held up relatively well, falling just 0.4%
today (chart).
Neoclouds also tumbled, with CoreWeave down 6.7% and Nebius down
16.8%, amid concerns about NVIDIA-backed financing (chart). Nebius
was additionally pressured by disappointing revenue guidance and rising
capital spending.

### Page 4

Despite the latest AI jitters, the two-year Treasury yield rose 15bps to
4.87% this evening, remaining well above the federal funds rate and
signaling that further Fed tightening is warranted (chart). The 10-year
Treasury yield is back above 5.00% at 5.13% this evening.
Yields rose on higher oil prices, with Brent crude up 3.1% to $95.66 this
evening, after President Donald Trump dismissed the need for an Iran
deal, and on hawkish Fed commentary following October's hot CPI report

(chart).

### Page 5

Markets now price a 100% probability of a December Fed rate hike:
84.7% for 25bps and 15.3% for 50bps. Futures imply more than 3 rate
hikes over the next 12 months (chart).
The combination of higher Treasury yields, driven by rising oil prices and
hawkish Fed commentary, and renewed AI jitters weighed on the broader
stock market. The S&P 500 fell 0.4%, its equal-weighted counterpart
declined 0.9%, and the Russell 2000 lost 1.7% (charts).

### Page 6

II. Nvidia: The Bear Case
We can assess the risks Jensen Huang's departure poses to the broader
AI trade with our familiar framework of three constraints on the AI
investment boom.
(1) Financial constraints. NVIDIA has been described by many as the
central bank of the AI trade, conducting its own version of quantitative
easing by helping finance the expansion of AI computing capacity. The
company spent $42.2 billion purchasing equity securities during the first

half of its current fiscal year, compared with just $1.2 billion a year earlier.
The fear is that Huang's departure could slow or even end these financing
activities, tightening financial conditions for an industry already facing
questions about the returns on its enormous capital expenditures.

### Page 7

(2) Political constraints. Huang has also been the AI trade's biggest
cheerleader, tirelessly promoting its economic benefits and defending key
players. During October's AI jitters, for example, he publicly defended
CoreWeave's financial health amid a selloff in its shares and bonds,
helping restore confidence. His departure could deprive the industry of its
most influential advocate and cheerleader.
Additionally, NVIDIA reportedly intervened in the Commerce Department's

investigation into Megaspeed, while Commerce Secretary Howard Lutnick
reportedly resisted efforts to blacklist the company, which remains off the
Entity List. Reports also suggest the Trump administration knew about
Huang's relationship with a suspected Chinese spy. These allegations
could invite tougher congressional oversight and undermine Trump's
national security argument for building more data centers in the US,
making the AI buildout harder to sell to the public.

(3) Physical constraints. Washington could respond by tightening chip
export controls and closing loopholes that allow Chinese companies to
access NVIDIA-powered computing capacity remotely. If so, China might
retaliate by restricting exports of rare earth minerals, some of which are
crucial for building data centers. China has already warned Washington
against using the controversy as a pretext for imposing tougher
technology restrictions.
III. Nvidia: Our Base Case

Our base case is that NVIDIA's financing strategy remains intact. Colette
Kress, NVIDIA's longtime CFO and newly appointed interim CEO, has
worked closely with Huang since 2013 and helped oversee the company's
strategic investments and financing arrangements. During NVIDIA's
August 26 earnings call, she defended these investments against
accusations of circular financing, stating, "We recognize the scale of this
support, and we know some will call it circular financing. We see it

differently." She argued that these investments would generate attractive
returns while strengthening NVIDIA's AI ecosystem, suggesting continuity
under her leadership. We don't expect political constraints to meaningfully
increase as a result of the controversy. Nor do we expect another trade
conflict with China to erupt.

### Page 8

Even if some of the constraints intensify, they would likely turn existing
speed bumps into bigger speed bumps rather than roadblocks to the AI
investment boom. AI remains the latest evolution in the Digital Revolution
that began with IBM's mainframes, and we expect the investment boom to
continue without Huang (chart). We therefore view any resulting selloff as
another buying opportunity for the AI trade.

IET-KY (4971 TT)

調降評等 
中立(Neutral) 
展望 2027年營運正向，雙位數成長可期  
⚫ 3Q26毛利率低於預期，磷化銦產品比重下滑 
⚫ 2027年基板供應將改善，磷化銦產品仍為重要成長動能 
⚫ 砷化鎵光電產品持續開發，有望成為新營運動能 
3Q26財報低於預期：公司於 2026年 11月 12日舉行第三季法說會，
3Q26 營收 3.19 億元(QoQ+24.64%，YoY+26.84%)、毛利率 38.72% 
(QoQ+4.72ppts，YoY+7.05ppts)、營業利益率 18.16 %(QoQ+9.73ppts，
YoY+4.75ppts)、稅後淨利 5,933 萬元(QoQ+214%，YoY+98%)、單季
1.26元 EPS。3Q26營收及毛利率低於我們預期，主要因磷化銦基板供
應仍受限制，德系基板供應商產能爬坡進展不如預期，而主要日系供
應商供貨份額未有太大改變，使高毛利磷化銦產品營收佔比自前季
48.7%下滑至 33.4%，且砷化鎵產品營收占比因相關射頻及國防訂單顯
著上升，影響產品組合。3Q26營業費用 0.65億元大約符合我們預期，
因公司持續投注研發費用於專業機台進行較高階產品的研發，費用較
往年明顯提升。 
磷化銦磊晶片仍是 2027年主要成長動能：2027年成長動能仍來自磷
化銦磊晶片，雖 3Q26供應狀況未如我們先前預期，公司認為磷化銦
基板供應於 4Q26已有些微放緩跡象，展望 2027年供應限制有望進一
步改善，預期毛利率將回升至四成以上水準。2027年公司展望營收成
長約 10%至 25%，新興 APD及 HBT磊晶產品也預計於 2027年量產，
而除磷化銦外，4Q26起砷化鎵及銻化鎵國防與商用訂單顯著成長，其
中銻化鎵紅外磊晶片已加速訂單需求，預期 2027 年訂單持續擴大，
並增加產能。磷化銦基板供應未如先前所預期顯著改善以及公司明年
展望狀況，我們下修 2027年營收及獲利預估。 
砷化鎵光電產品靜待多點開花：為降低對傳統磷化銦基板供應的依
賴，公司積極開發 QD Laser、變晶型 PIN等新產品切入光通訊市場，
其中，QD Laser 已受到客戶初步良好反饋，進行送樣當中。 200G 
VCSEL方面也持續進行研發，並已與客戶配合 NRE。 
受股數稀釋獲利及市場影響下調評價：公司與光聖進行股份交換使流
通在外股數增加致流通在外股數上升，我們預估 2026、2027年 EPS 
分別為 5.08、7.66元，此外，考量近期市場評價有較大幅度修正，較
前次報告下調本益比，我們基於 2027預估 EPS x 50倍本益比，目標
價為 383元，調降至中立之投資評等建議。風險因子：基板供應短缺。

► 美國9月CPI年增率上升至3.7%，核心CPI年
增率由2.4%升至2.6%，核心月增率0.3%，
高於預期值0.2%。能源是通膨反彈的主因，
貢獻整體CPI月增幅超過一半；能源衝擊雖
為主導，但消費韌性使企業保有成本轉嫁能
力，住房與部分服務價格維持黏性，需求面
通膨略有提高，加上企業資本支出提升經濟
與企業獲利韌性，使總需求不易快速降溫。
► 凱投預期Fed 12月將升息1碼，明年3月再升
1碼，政策利率可能在4%以上維持更久。受
到油價高盤、通膨僵固，與財政惡化、政府
發債籌資逢企業債搶錢等影響，美歐日主要
國家公 債利 率近 期 來到高 位 。其中， 法國
2027年財政預算欲刪減支出(包括養老金、公
部門工資等)引發民眾反彈，衝擊主權信用評
等，法國CDS與法德10年期公債利差擴大、
歐元走弱，美元轉強，商品、黃金承壓。法
國政治不確定性讓歐股修正、法股最疲，成
熟國家公債殖利率升高，增加企業借貸成本
與股市評價壓力，續為金融市場擾動因子。

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，Goldman Sachs，凱基證券整理，註：右上圖之大型雲端服務供應商(CSP)企業為Amazon、Alphabet、Microsoft、Oracle
► 美國第三季財報季展開，市場將觀察企業在
高利率環境下的獲利表現。根據FactSet數據
顯示，標普500指數第三季獲利成長預估達
29.5%，顯示企業基本面仍具支撐，其中科
技 產業 受 惠AI需求帶動， 獲 利 成 長預 估達
65%，持續扮演企業獲利的重要成長動能。
► 聚焦科技類股，AI資本支出仍為本輪企業獲
利成長的主要驅動力。高盛指出，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面。隨財報
數據陸續公布，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現，並維持正向財測，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。
美企財報獲利穩健，對股市中長期有撐

評價面疑慮下，股價難以進一步擴張

投資焦點
► 醫療保健、醫療服務與生物技術等行業，在
景氣不好時，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天，股市波動風險上升，觀察
過去當VIX恐慌指數上揚時，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑，部分公司估值偏低。當
臨床數據改善、或導入AI篩選資料與藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值，帶動整體產業估值回升。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場需求，
創新藥物(如瘦瘦針)讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場2030年銷售額預估上調15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。
醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健

市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響，由於商業雇主
贊助保險(ESI)和平價醫療法案(ACA)的自付
額增加，以及最高自付費用要求，較去年同
期提高，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限)來擴大利潤空間，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險(ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去20 年來的最高水準。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長，市場靜待財報結果與展望，和對AI資本支出回收影響。
考量地緣衝突、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉，短期金融市場紛擾不止，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變，逢股市拉回時可分批布局，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點，全球利率走揚有利銀行股。此外，地緣衝突頻傳，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間，殖利率偏高環境使短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等

匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高，使金價短期承壓，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 370: Q3 Growth in advanced economies_ solid despite shocks (2026-10-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Q3 Growth in advanced economies_ solid despite shocks (2026-10-23).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 23 October 2026

EcoFlash

Q3 Growth in advanced economies: solid despite shocks

Growth looks to have held up in Q3 despite successive shocks (see our analysis), as it did in 2025 and H1 2026. A longer 
Middle East conflict has fed into more persistent inflation; drought has worsened in many countries, and interest rates 
have risen further – at the short end, with both the ECB and the Fed raising policy rates in September, and even more at 
the long end. However, our nowcasts show that growth is bending without breaking in most advanced economies. GDP 
figures (due on 29 October in the US and 30 October for the euro area and its member states) are expected to confirm 
that. In the Euro area, momentum looks stable (excluding Ireland, whose GDP is volatile). In Germany, growth has been 
uninterrupted since Q4 2025, while France is likely to have returned to growth after stagnating in H1. In the US, growth 
is likely to have gathered pace, according to the GDP Now, still driven by AI, but increasingly by other sectors as well.

Euro area: resilience beneath the surface. Our nowcast points to growth slowing to 0.3% in q/q, though the pace is stable 
excluding Ireland (Irish GDP is volatile and had markedly boosted the Q2 figure). Excluding Ireland, growth should hold at its H2 
average, a sign of the bloc’s underlying resilience. Also, momentum has broadened to a growing number of sectors (see our 
analysis). While industrial production stalled over the summer, services remained buoyant. The services PMI rebounded sharply 
in Q3 and sits firmly in expansion territory (53.0 in September). Improving job-related survey data (PMI and European Commission 
surveys) and a stable unemployment rate (6.4% in August) hint at more hiring in Q3 – a support for household consumption that 
partly offsets purchasing-power losses from persistent inflation. Corporate investment is underpinned by buoyant new investment 
lending (8.7% y/y In August, 12-month cumulative). 
France: growing, nevertheless. The succession of shocks does not appear to have prevented France from returning to 
growth in Q3, at 0.2% q/q, according to our nowcast. One-off factors accounted for the stall in activity in H1. In Q3, the drought 
worsened and industrial production remained subdued. However, market services production rebounded in July and August, 
notably in business services, restaurants, digital and real estate. The latter points to residential investment recovering after a weak 
H1. Corporate investment likely followed, as reflected in buoyant credit flows and AI-related investment. Household consumption 
is likely to have improved slightly on higher electric vehicle sales.  
Euro area  France

Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas  Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas

Germany: growth still solid. The H1 rebound in activity is moderating but continues. Our nowcast points to a fourth straight 
quarter of growth in Q3, at 0.2% q/q (after 0.3% in Q4 2025, 0.4% in Q1 2026, and 0.3% in Q2). Manufacturing activity eased 
slightly, according to the September PMI survey (53.9, down 0.4 m/m). Government spending will remain growth-supportive, with 
defence and infrastructure outlays gaining traction. Infrastructure spending continues to underpin activity in public works, which 
stands at its highest since November 2021 (2.5% 3m/3m in August). Exports (4.4% y/y between January and August) remain the 
main growth engine, helped by a pivot towards Europe (7.2%). 
United States: an expansion undeterred by inflation. Growth is likely to have gathered pace in Q3. The Atlanta Fed’s GDP 
Now points to a 3.5% annualized growth rate (AR), close to our own 3.7% forecast (0.9% non-AR) and up from 2.2% in 
Q2. Domestic demand remains the engine. Consumption is weathering the pickup in inflation, while the AI-driven capex cycle 
remains robust – as reflected in retail sales and nondefence capital-goods orders (excl. aircraft). As a result, growth in final sales

### Page 2

to private domestic purchasers1 should print above 4% AR again. By contrast, external trade should remain a drag on growth, 
reflecting AI-related import needs. Survey data point to momentum that is both solid and broad-based. The ISM indices have 
remained firmly in growth territory (54.9 quarterly average for manufacturing and services alike). At the same time, they highlight 
persistent price and supply-chain pressures.

Germany   United States

Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas   Source: Refinitiv, Atlanta Fed, BNP Paribas

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of the Advanced Economies Team, France 
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Senior Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

Lucie Barette 
Economist, Germany 
lucie.barette@bnpparibas.com

Anis Bensaidani 
Economist, United States 
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com

Tarik Rharrab 
Nowcast Methodology 
 tarik.rharrab@bnpparibas.com

1 The sum of household consumption and private fixed investment.

## Document 422: What Taiwan semi_tech Oct sales tell us about global tech outlook

Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    陸⾏之  Andrew Lu on global semis and techs <andrewhclu@substack.com>
Subject: What Taiwan semi/tech Oct sales tell us about global tech outlook
Date:    2 hours ago
What Taiwan semi/tech Oct sales tell us about global tech outlook

What Taiwan semi/tech Oct
sales tell us about global tech
outlook
從 10 ⽉份台灣科技及半導體營收數字來趨吉避凶
陸⾏之  ANDREW LU ON SEMI/TECHS
NOV 10 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
Executive summary
在經過 9-10 ⽉全球半導體及科技股估值超過 30% 的技術修正以來，投資⼈開

始擔⼼ AI 數據算⼒中⼼基礎建設在明後年是否能持續，雖然市場早已經跳過 10
⽉份及 4Q26 營收看未來需求，但我們認為 10 ⽉份⾼達 8 個產業的營收明顯加
速仍相對重要，除了 AI 數據算⼒中⼼產業鏈持續表現不俗之外，蘋果⼿機產業
鏈也有季節性復甦，但 PC 產業鏈就明顯地⼀蹶不振。除此之外，我們分享 1 家
居然在⾼預期下仍超標的公司，並建議投資⼈避開 2 家在低預期下仍  miss 的

（注）コンセンサスは Bloomberg 及び THOMSON REUTERS 調査。灰色で囲んでいる指標は本レポートで解説を行っています。 
[インド]～生活必需品の価格上昇、ルピー安や価格転嫁を背景にインフレ圧力が広がり、目標を上回る～ 
12 日に発表された 10 月の消費者物価は前年同月比＋6.09％となり、前月（同＋5.54％）から伸
びが加速し、2024 年 10 月以来２年ぶりにＲＢＩ（インド準備銀行）が定める物価目標レンジ（４
±２％）の上限を上回った。前月比は＋0.51％と前月（同＋0.69％）から上昇ペースこそ鈍化した
ものの、物価の上昇基調は続いている。原油価格の高止まりがエネルギー関連価格への上昇圧力と
なっているほか、生鮮品や穀物などの食料品価格も上昇するなど、生活必需品を中心にインフレ圧
力が強まっている。なお、食料品とエネルギーを除いたコアインフレ率も前年同月比＋4.51％とな
り、前月（同＋4.33％）から伸びが加速して、３ヵ月連続で物価目標の中心値である４％を上回っ
て推移している。前月比も＋0.30％と前月（同＋0.40％）に続いて上昇しており、エネルギー価格
の上昇に伴う輸送コストの増大に加え、金融市場でのルピー安の進行による輸入物価の上昇も重な
り、幅広い財価格に上昇圧力が及んでいる。さらに、サービス価格についても上昇基調を強める動
きが確認されており、企業によるコスト増加分の価格転嫁が広がるなか、インフレ圧力が一段と強
まっている様子がうかがえる。

### Page 2

2/4

[台湾]～半導体関連をけん引役に 10 月輸出額は過去最高を更新、輸入額、貿易黒字幅も過去最大に～ 
９日に発表された 10 月の輸出額は前年同月比＋58.9％となり、前月（同＋60.9％）から伸びが
鈍化した。前月比は＋10.9％と前月（同＋7.6％）から拡大ペースが加速しており、単月ベースの輸
出額は過去最高を更新した。財別では、世界的なＡＩ（人工知能）・半導体関連需要の旺盛さを反映
して、主力輸出財である半導体などの電子部品や情報通信・映像関連製品の輸出が引き続き拡大し
ている。一方、プラスチック関連や化学製品関連などの輸出は相対的に伸びが弱く、分野ごとにば
らつきが生じている。国・地域別では、米国や欧州、日本など先進国向けが引き続き拡大基調で推
移しているほか、中国本土やＡＳＥＡＮ（東南アジア諸国連合）向けも堅調な動きをみせており、
幅広い地域で輸出の拡大が続いている。一方、輸入額は前年同月比＋75.1％となり、前月（同＋
51.7％）から伸びが加速した。前月比も＋8.3％と前月（同＋7.9％）から拡大ペースが加速してお
り、単月ベースの輸入額も過去最高を更新した。半導体関連を中心とする輸出の堅調さを反映して、
生産能力の増強に必要な設備をはじめとする機械製品関連の輸入が押し上げられるとともに、化学
製品関連やプラスチック関連など素材・部材関連の輸入も堅調に推移しており、輸出拡大が輸入の
増加を促す構図が続いている。その結果、貿易収支は＋278.75 億ドルと前月（＋236.30 億ドル）
から黒字幅が拡大し、単月ベースで過去最大となった。

[韓国]～10 月失業率は小幅に改善するも、若年層を中心に雇用環境は厳しさを増す展開が続く～ 
11 日に発表された10 月の失業率（季調済）は 2.5％となり、前月（2.6％）から 0.1pt 改善した。
失業者数は前月比▲2.9 万人と前月に続いて３ヵ月連続で減少しており、中期的にも減少基調で推

移するなど調整の動きが続いている。年代別では、60 代以上の高齢層で増加する動きがみられるも
のの、20 代や 30 代、40 代での減少が全体を下押ししている。一方、就業者数は前月比▲5.0 万人
と前月の増加から５ヵ月ぶりの減少に転じており、雇用拡大の動きに一服感が出ている。年代別で
は、60 代以上の高齢層で増加する動きがみられる一方、10 代や 20 代といった若年層での減少が雇
用全体の重しとなっている。雇用形態別では、常用雇用に底堅い動きがみられるものの、臨時・日
雇い雇用を中心に調整の動きが確認されている。労働力人口も前月比▲0.8 万人減と前月に続いて
減少しており、年代別では、60 代以上の高齢層で増加する一方、10 代や 20 代など若年層を中心に
減少するなど、労働市場から退出する動きが広がっている。こうした動きを反映して、労働参加率
（季調済）は 64.4％と前月（64.6％）から 0.2pt 低下しており、表面的には失業率が改善している
ものの、雇用を取り巻く環境は厳しさを増していると捉えられる。

[マレーシア]～世界的な AI・半導体関連需要の旺盛さを追い風に、製造業を中心に生産拡大が続く～ 
10 日に発表された９月の鉱工業生産は前年同月比＋6.3％となり、前月（同＋5.0％）から伸びが
加速した。前月比も＋1.19％と前月（同＋0.54％）から２ヵ月連続で拡大しており、足元の生産活
動は持ち直しの動きをみせている。分野別では、原油や天然ガスなど鉱業部門の生産に下押し圧力
がかかる一方、製造業の生産は前月比で２ヵ月ぶりの拡大に転じるなど持ち直しの動きを強めてい
る。また、経済活動の動向に連動する発電量も前年同月比で拡大しており、製造業を中心に生産活
動が活発化している様子がうかがえる。製造業のなかでは、世界的なＡＩ（人工知能）・半導体関連
需要の旺盛さを反映して、半導体など電子部品関連や電気機械関連の生産が軒並み拡大している。
さらに、自動車など輸送用機械関連の生産も前年同月比で拡大に転じるとともに、金属関連や化学
製品関連など素材・部材関連の生産にも底堅い動きがみられる。

## Document 504: 中共５中全会閉幕、次期党大会を見据えて党・軍への統制強化を加速 ～経済政策の実効性と日中関係の行方にも注目～

Source PDF: all report pdf/中共５中全会閉幕、次期党大会を見据えて党・軍への統制強化を加速 ～経済政策の実効性と日中関係の行方にも注目～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 30 日

1/6 
中共５中全会閉幕、次期党大会を見据えて党・軍への統制強化を加速 
～経済政策の実効性と日中関係の行方にも注目～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 中国では、10 月26～29 日に中国共産党の第 20 期中央委員会第５回全体会議（５中全会）が
開催された。今回は、従来の慣例とは異なり、「全面的かつ厳格な党内統治」が主要議題と
なり、党規律や幹部の監督、反腐敗の強化、党中央による政策履行の徹底などを盛り込んだ
決定が採択された。また、軍幹部を含む多数の党籍剥奪処分を追認するなど、党・軍に対す
る統制強化の姿勢が鮮明となった。さらに、2027 年後半に第 21 回党大会を開催することが
正式に決定され、習近平指導部は次期党大会に向けて組織・人事面での基盤固めを進める方
針を示したと考えられる。 
○ 一方で、経済政策面では、内需拡大や景気安定に向けた方針が再確認されたものの、具体的
な追加刺激策は示されなかった。足元の中国経済は、外需の堅調さを背景に持ち直しの動き
がみられる一方、不動産不況や消費・投資の低迷など、内需の弱さが依然として課題となっ
ている。こうしたなか、今後は既存の景気支援策の実効性が焦点となるほか、党内統治の厳
格化が行政の政策遂行能力を高めるのか、一方で幹部の責任回避などによる政策執行の遅れ
を招くのかが注目される。 
○ 日本への影響については、中国の内需回復が日本企業の輸出や現地販売を下支えする可能性
がある一方、中国政府による国産化や産業育成の推進が競争圧力を強めることも懸念される。
さらに、重要部材の輸出管理強化や台湾情勢を巡る安全保障上の不確実性が、日本企業の調
達や事業活動に及ぼす影響にも注意が必要となる。今後は、2027 年の次期党大会に向けた組
織・人事の動向に加え、景気支援策の具体的な執行状況、地方行政の対応、対外関係の変化
などを通じて、習近平指導部の政策運営の方向性を見極める必要がある。

【今回の５中全会では、党内統治の強化と政策の履行徹底が主要議題となった】 
中国では、10 月 26 日から 29 日までの日程で中国共産党の第 20 期中央委員会第５回全体会議
（５中全会）が開催された。党大会の閉会期間中に党の指導機関としての役割を担う中央委員会は、
５年の任期中にほぼ１年ごとに１回全体会議を開催し、党の統治や運営上の重要政策、人事などの
議題を討議する。１中全会では新指導部が正式に発足し、２中全会では国務院（政府）などの人事

### Page 2

2/6 
案を承認し、３中全会では経済政策を討議するなど、２中全会と３中全会は通常、党大会の翌年に
開催される。しかし、習近平指導部は３中全会を 2023 年に開催せず、2024 年７月に後ずれした（注
１）。さらに、2025 年 10 月に開始した４中全会では、第 15 次５ヵ年計画（十五五）が討議された
（注２）。 
その結果、通常は５ヵ年計画を討議する場となる５中全会で、今回は「全面的かつ厳格な党内統
治（全面従厳治党）」が主要議題となり、実質的に党大会の前年に開催される６中全会に近い性格を
帯びることとなった。このように、習近平指導部の下では、党の会議の開催時期や議題などについ
て、過去の慣例にとらわれない動きがみられる。 
会議では、習氏が中央政治局を代表して行った活動報告を聴取・討議するとともに、「全面的かつ
厳格な党内統治を持続的に推進するうえでの若干の重大問題に関する決定」を採択した。同決定に
は、党規律、幹部の選抜・監督、組織運営、反腐敗の強化といった内容が盛り込まれた。会議後に
公表された公報によれば、中央の指示を地方や各部門まで徹底するとともに、政治的な忠誠と実務
上の遂行能力を幹部管理に反映させる姿勢がうかがえる。反腐敗を長期的な執政の基盤と位置付け
たうえで、個々の処分にとどまらず、組織的な監督と責任追及の仕組みを整える狙いがあると考え
られる。 
この方向性を考えるうえでは、会議に先立って党の理論誌『求是』に掲載された習氏の演説が重
要な手がかりとなる。これは１月に行われた演説の主要部分を公表したものであり、このなかで習
氏は、党中央の決定が実行されない、または恣意的に実行される問題を挙げるとともに、政治的な
監督の強化の必要性を訴えた。このため、今回の会議においても、政治的忠誠と政策履行の徹底、
反腐敗と監督の制度化、幹部の評価・登用の厳格化が重視されたと考えられる。 
人事面では、中央軍事委員会の前副主席である張又侠氏や元委員の劉振立氏など 33 人に対する
党籍剥奪処分を追認したほか、別の１人に対する党内職務の罷免処分を決定した。党籍剥奪の対象
者は、習近平指導部の下で開催された中央委員会全体会議として過去最多の規模とみられ、うち16
人は元軍関係者が占めた。軍幹部を巡っては、ここ数年の相次ぐ処分が指揮命令系統の弱体化を招
くと懸念されてきた。このため、中央軍事委員会の欠員補充が注目されたものの、今回は同委員会
副主席の張昇民氏を中央政治局委員に追加選出するにとどまった。軍の規律検査を担ってきた張氏
を昇格させることで、次期党大会に向けて軍への統制を強化し、指導体制の立て直しを進める方針
が示されたと考えられる。とはいえ、軍の指揮命令系統を巡る空洞化の問題は残されたままである。
一方、今回は中央委員 24 人が党籍剥奪処分の対象となるなか、中央候補委員 10 人を中央委員に繰
り上げるなど、組織体制の補強も図られた。こうした動きを踏まえれば、今回の５中全会は党・軍
への引き締めを一段と強化する会議となったと捉えられる。

注１ 2024 年７月 19 日付レポート「中国･３中全会､習近平氏が掲げる｢中国式現代化｣の加速を推進」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/351237.html） 
注２ 2025 年10 月24 日付レポート「中共４中全会閉幕､中長期的な国力向上の姿勢が鮮明に」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/530799.html）

### Page 3

3/6

【2027 年に開催予定の次期党大会に向けて、組織・人事面での基盤整備に注力】 
５中全会では、中国共産党第 21 回全国代表大会（第 21 回党大会）を 2027 年後半に北京で開催
することを正式に決定した。これにより、今後行われる中央・地方の幹部人事や政策運営を評価す
るうえでは、次期党大会に向けた準備という視点がこれまで以上に重要になる。 
５中全会での決定事項を踏まえれば、政治的忠誠と政策遂行能力の重視という観点が、次の指導
部を担う幹部の評価と選抜に関わると考えられる。次期党大会に向けて地方・中央の人事が進めら
れる過程では、党中央の方針をどれだけ着実に実行できるかが問われることになろう。ただし、今
回行われた中央委員の補充は党規約に定められた手続きに沿ったものであり、今回補充された 10
人がそのまま後継指導者候補とはならないことに注意が必要である。 
さらに、思想面から現行指導体制の継続を支える動きも重要であろう。具体的には、公報におい
て、毛沢東思想や鄧小平理論など従来の指導思想に言及するなかで「習近平党建設思想」が明記さ
れ、習近平「新時代の中国の特色ある社会主義」思想の重要な構成要素と位置付けられたことは、
同指導部による党運営の理念を定着させる動きと捉えられる。同思想は、「人民の利益を重視する
（人民至上）」立場を基本としつつ、党統治の厳格化、反腐敗運動の徹底、民主集中制の強化など、
今回の５中全会で討議された内容と重なる要素が多い。なお、この概念は 2026 年６月に開催され
た全国党建設活動座談会で正式に提起・体系化されており、今回初めて登場したものではない。今
後は、次期党大会の政治報告や党規約などにどのような形で反映されるかが注目される。 
前述したように、５中全会では多数の軍幹部が処分されたものの、これは規律による統制を徹底
する姿勢と、人事面での再整備を続ける必要性の双方を示唆している。今回、中央政治局委員に選
出された張升民氏は軍の規律検査を担ってきた経歴を有しており、習近平指導部が人民解放軍に対
する政治的統制を重視してきた姿勢と整合的である。一方で、軍人事の変更だけで軍の実戦能力や
対外行動を直接判断することは難しい。習氏は、台湾統一を目的とする武力行使を選択肢から排除
しておらず、米軍に対抗し得る『世界一流の軍隊』の建設を目指してきた。2027 年８月には人民解
放軍の創設 100 周年を迎えることから、それまでに軍の指揮命令系統の再構築が進めば、同年後半
の党大会で４期目入りを目指すとみられる習氏にとって、権力基盤の一層の安定につながる可能性
がある。

【経済政策面では具体策に乏しいものの、景気安定に向けた政策の実行力が焦点に】 
一方で、経済政策との関係では、十五五の策定過程との区別が必要である。十五五に関する「建
議」は４中全会で採択されるとともに、それを具体化した「綱要」は 2026 年３月の全人代（第 14
期全国人民代表大会第４回全体会議）で承認されている（注３）。このため、今回の５中全会は、こ
うした既存の政策を着実に遂行する組織・幹部の規律を整えるための会議と位置付けられる。

注３ ３月５日付レポート「中国､2026 年全人代開幕､特異性を強める経済､内政､外交」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/581059.html）

### Page 4

4/6 
こうしたなか、公報では、マクロ政策の活用と内需拡大を求めるとともに、2026 年の経済・社会
発展目標を達成する方針を再確認した。一方で、追加財政支出の規模や利下げ幅、新たな家計給付
の金額など、具体的な政策措置は示されなかった。今後の政策運営の動きについては、政府や中国
人民銀行（中銀）をはじめとする金融当局が発表する政策の内容に加え、その執行状況などを確認
したうえで評価する必要があると考えられる。 
足元の中国経済を巡っては、経済成長率に持ち直しの動きがみられる一方、内需は力強さを欠く
推移をみせている。７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前年同期比＋4.5％と前期（同＋4.3％）から小幅
に加速したほか、前期比年率ベースでは＋4.9％と試算されるなど持ち直しの動きが確認されてい
る（注４）。 一方で、９月までの累計ベースの小売売上高（社会消費品小売総額）は前年比＋1.1％
にとどまり、固定資産投資は同▲7.2％とマイナスで推移するとともに、なかでも不動産投資は同
▲20.6％と大幅に下振れしている。こうしたなか、足元の景気は外需の旺盛な動きを追い風にした
ハイテク製造業を中心とする供給サイドがけん引役となる展開が続いており、国内の需給不均衡が
解消されない状況にある。国家統計局も、内需の弱さを経済運営上の課題として認識しており、景
気の回復力にはなお弱さが残ると考えられる。 
このため、金融市場では、2027 年後半に開催される党大会を前に、景気や雇用、社会の安定を維
持する観点から、何らかの政策対応が強化されるとの見方が根強い。とはいえ、内需の低迷が長期
化しているにもかかわらず、これまで大規模な景気刺激策に積極姿勢をみせてこなかったことを勘
案すれば、政治日程のみを材料に積極姿勢に転じると見込むことは難しい。国務院は７月、同国で
初めて個人消費に焦点を当てた「消費拡大の５ヵ年計画」を公表した。同計画では、2030 年の小売
売上高（社会消費品小売総額）の規模を 2025 年（50.1 兆元）に比べて約２割（60 兆元前後）引き
上げるべく、消費構造の高度化や家計所得の引き上げを目指すとした。加えて、共産党は７月末に
開催した中央政治局会議において、インフラ関連など財政支出の進捗加速による景気下支えを図る
方針を示した。これを受けて、政府・金融当局は８月以降、内需促進を目的とする新たな財政・金
融支援策を発表しており、今後はこれらの政策がどの程度の効果を上げるかを見定める必要がある。 
一方で、今後焦点となり得るのが、党内統治の厳格化と政策実行力の関係であろう。汚職や形式
的な行政対応が減るとともに、党中央・政府が提示した内需支援策が適切に執行されれば、政策の
実効性が高まることが期待される。ただし、幹部が責任追及を警戒して判断を先送りするほか、上
位機関への照会を増やすような動きが広がれば、企業の許認可や政策資金の執行が遅れる可能性は
残る。今回の決定のみをもって政策効果の行方を判断することはできず、地方レベルでの行政実務
の動向を注視する必要があろう。その意味では、政策の規模に加え、資金の使途と執行状況を点検
することが重要になる。仮に、今後も供給サイドの改革が優先されるとともに、産業設備投資への
支援が集中すれば、需要に見合わない生産能力の増強を招くとともに、国内における価格競争や対
外摩擦を強める可能性に注意が必要となる。

注４ 10 月19 日付レポート「中国・７-９月 GDP は前年比＋4.5％に加速も、外需依存の構図は変わらず」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666711.html）

### Page 5

5/6

【日本への影響を巡っては、中国の内需動向、行政運営、産業競争、供給網などの動向に注意】 
日本にとって中国は、需要先であると同時に重要な供給元でもある。2025 年時点において、日本
の貿易に占める中国の割合は、輸出で 17.0％、輸入で 23.6％となっている。中国の需要動向は日
本の輸出や現地販売に、供給面の変化は日本の調達や生産活動に影響を及ぼす。このため、５中全
会で示された党内統治の強化の行方が、今後どのような形で政策運営や行政実務に反映されるかが
重要になる。 
まず、内需の行方は、中国に進出する日系企業の販売機会に直結すると予想される。当局が主導
する家計所得や雇用への支援が実効性を持つことができれば、消費関連の日系企業の現地販売のほ
か、日本からの輸出を支えることが期待される。一方で、産業設備への投資が中心となる場合には、
機械・電子部品・素材などに需要が向かいやすい。ただし、支援対象、現地調達条件、日本企業の
競争力によって恩恵は異なると予想されるほか、中国の景気対策の規模がそのまま日本の輸出増に
つながるとは限らない点には注意が必要である。 
次に、行政運営が事業環境に及ぼす影響にも注意が必要となる。規律強化が恣意的な行政や不透
明な取引を減らせば、企業の事業環境に対する予見性を高める効果が期待できる。反対に、担当者
の交代や責任追及への警戒が判断の遅れを招く場合には、事業認可、政府調達、契約履行などに支
障が生じ得る。中国日本商会が発表した 2026 年白書においても、行政手続の迅速化、制度運用の
統一、内外企業の公平な競争条件の確保などが求められている。日本企業にとっては、こうした改
善が実務レベルでどの程度進展するかが重要な判断材料になる。 
さらに、中国国内における産業育成に伴う需要と競争環境の行方にも注意が必要となる。習近平
指導部が掲げる科学技術の自立自強やサプライチェーンの強靱化、「新質生産力」の育成といった
方針は、すでに十五五の策定段階で重視されている。今後、こうした動きが一段と強力に推進され
れば、日本の設備・部材メーカーに販売機会をもたらすことが期待される一方、国産化が進んだ製
品については中期的に競争圧力が強まる可能性がある。すでに、太陽光電池や風力発電設備につい
ては、中国メーカーが世界市場で高いシェアを有するほか、ＥＶ（電気自動車）でも高い国際競争
力を持つ。中国市場における需要の行方だけでなく、日本企業の技術や製品が代替される可能性の
ほか、第三国市場での競争激化が一段と加速する可能性にも注意が必要である。さらに、世界的に
ＡＩ（人工知能）・半導体を巡る開発競争が激化するなか、７月には中国が主導して世界ＡＩ機構
（WAICO）という国際機関を設立するなど、この分野をリードする姿勢を強めており、その動向にも
注意が必要である。 
足元で特に注視すべきなのが、重要部材の供給と安全保障を巡る影響であろう。このところの中
国は、高市首相による台湾有事を巡る国会答弁や日本の防衛政策に対する反発を強めるなか、日本
に対する「経済的威圧」を強めている。中国商務部は１月､日本に対する軍民両用品（デュアルユー
ス）の輸出管理を強化する方針を発表し、その後も対象を拡大するなど、規制を強化してきた。足
元では、レアアース関連の輸出許可や通関手続きの運用を巡る不確実性が、企業実務上の重要な課
題となっている。今後も、対象品目や審査基準に関する当局の個別公告に加え、企業による輸出許

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
可の取得や調達の動向を確認する必要があることは間違いない。 
安全保障面についても、軍幹部の処分や政治局人事をもって、台湾有事の時期や可能性を判断す
ることは困難である。日本への影響については、台湾海峡や東シナ海における実際の軍事活動、日
中間の外交対話、経済措置の変化を通じて判断する必要がある。企業にとっては、物流、重要部材
の調達、現地拠点の運営にどのような支障が生じ得るかを、平時から具体的に点検することの重要
性がこれまで以上に高まっていると判断できる。

【今後は、政策の実施内容と現場レベルでの反応を確認する必要性が高まっている】 
先行きについては、現行路線の継続と党大会に向けた景気安定重視が基本線となる一方、行政の
実行力や対外関係の行方によって実際の展開が左右されると考えられる。とりわけ、幹部人事と次
期指導部の形成過程、家計支援・不動産対策・産業支援の具体的内容と執行状況、日系企業の許認
可・代金回収・調達の実態、輸出管理や外交・安全保障上の具体的な変化を継続的に確認する必要
性は高まっている。次期党大会で示される政治報告と、その後に開催される１中全会での指導部人
事の行方は、その先の政策運営を評価する重要な材料となる。その意味では、開催まで約１年とな
った次期党大会に向けて、党中央と地方組織双方の動きをこれまで以上に注視する必要があると考
えられる。

## Document 510: 中国製ハイブリッド車の輸入制限に踏み切るEU

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国製ハイブリッド車の輸入制限に踏み切るEU.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 20 日

MURC Focus

中国製ハイブリッド車の輸入制限に踏み切る EU

～日本にとっても“対岸の火事”ではない～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 12 月にセーフガードを発動 
欧州連合（EU）は 10 月 12 日、輸入が急増している中国製のハイブリッド車（HV）とプラグインハイブリッド車
（PHV）を対象に、12 月よりセーフガード（緊急輸入制限）を発動すると発表した。期間は 4 年間で、1 年目は HV
と PHV の年間輸入台数を計 35 万台とし、その後の増加幅は年間 6%までに制限する。EU 籍のメーカーが中
国で生産する車も対象となるが、中国メーカーの EU 域内での生産車は対象外となる。

EU は 2024 年 10 月 30 日より、中国製 EV（電気自動車）に対し、基本関税 10%に加えて、最大で 35.3%の
追加関税を課している。廉価な中国製 EV の輸入を抑制する目的からだが、一方で HV と PHV には追加関税
を課してこなかった。その結果、中国製 HV と PHV の輸入が 2025 年から急増し、EU 製の HV と PHV は劣勢
に追いやられた（図表 1）。また電動車の対中貿易収支も、HV と PHV を中心に赤字が膨らんだ（図表 2）。
